Early this morning around 4 am I was awoken by a humming noise right above my house, I
live in Oregon out in the boonies high elevation. I was still half a sleep so I didnt really hear
much besides the humming, it sounded like something real big and then it just stopped. It
hovered above my house for about 40 seconds or so, I didnt get up and go look outside
because like I said I was half awake and I didnt realize what it probably was until it was
gone.

On seeing this on here today I was shocked, I have never seen or heard a UFO before this
time and I am sure that is what it was....
Very weird !!!

every other fake or misleading case.

Always just a little too blurry, a little too far away, always some CCD light anomalies,
always jittery camera work. Never a clear multi megapixel focused image or video beyond
30 seconds following said "craft".

UFOs are real, but none of these are REAL ufos. Sure unidentified RC, Helicopter, Balloons
and unidentified digital camera anomalies.

The predictions from all the Charlatans & Charlottes have once again proven their worth in
a multiple of zero. The BONEHEADS who made the Oct 13th predictions lied, are culty
types and seemingly mentally disturbed as well as carelessly willing to cheapen their
military record/retirement. DISGUSTING! Most of all, they have something to sell, don't
they? Plus you are not to believe that woman or any other channeler unless I clear them
and say they are ok. (just a dig at our gorgeous "knots landing" friend with the chest full
of silica and the paralyzed nose & weakened septum who seemed to move a physics
doctorate from her spasmodic bowel)

UFOs are real, ALIENS are real. I know. I don't merely believe. But I haven't believed 0.01%
of anything I ever saw in a picture or a video and I'm being rather liberal about it. Every
prediction deadline brings forth a rowdy bar brawl of desperate forsaken believers. You
are not the first. I was taken in once, too. You'll come to be very angry at the time these
people leeched from you.

Stop trying to make it so people. It will never happen (alien revelation / non governmental
disclosure) as long as you think it will happen. There are no Pleidians nor Vril. Ron Hubbard
was an ass clown in a sailor's hat. You'll only feel vibrations from something using batteries
or AC power and the only frequency you see or hear will be from a TV or Radio. And on
those are the only "channels" you or I will ever know.

There is no love nor light amongst the dim. Just deceit and darkness.

posted on Oct, 14 2010 @ 05:29 AM                                                            link
" retired Air Force officer, Stanley A. Fulham, tentatively predicts October 13, 2010 as the
date for a massive UFO display over the world’s principal cities".

This is from his book and I watched the interview he did. Here is where I have a problem
with what may have happenend on the 13th.

1. was it a Massive UFO display? In my opinion, No.
2. was ufo activity recorded around the world's principal cities? in my opinion, No.

The fact that a few unidentified flying objects were seen in a few cities does not equate
"world's principal cities." This was supposed to be a massive showing where many crafts
would hover for hours over cities. He stated that there would be irrefutable proof of alien
existence. The fact that there were sightings on the 13th doesn't make his prediction
true. There has been an increase in reports of UFO's around the world for months now.

This was not the monumental event he predicted would occur on this date. This is my
perspective and you are welcome to disagree with it. It's important to view each sighting
on an individual basis and determine if it is real, hoax, cgi,or misidentification. I tend to
be warry of people giving out specific dates for the apocalypse, alien invasion, and
disclosure.

If a flotilla of alien crafts had positioned themselves over the world's principal cities for
hours, I would the first to be posting about such an amazing event.


Thanks.

picture of the last 48 hours.

The NORAD officer clearly had foreknowledge of the event, anyone trying to predict the
future is clearly going to have to take a general stab in the dark.

What was the core of his prediction? That there would be some event along the lines of
UFO activity that is out of the norm. This HAS occured. IMO.

I do have to add this, last night as my kids and I were in the yard picking up those dreaded
sticks, kitwas getting dark. I went inside to do some dishes the kids wanted to stay
outside and play. About 5 minutes later my youngest daughter comes rushing in, telling me
she say a shooting star. I asked her a few questions, about what it looked like where she
saw it, and how fats it was moving. She said it was moving slow,lights were twinkling on
it, and it disappeared. I was like okay that's odd. I couldn't stand in the yard and look up,
because I had a very stiff neck, and a headache yesterday.

So after them looking up at the stars for awhile more and being outside sitting on my
porch, I'm like let's go in. we head to the back yard to go in, and my youngest screams,
"There's the Star!" I was able to glance up and see a moving object that was indeed
twinkling and moving slowly. I was standing there for a minute going wait what is that?
Then I look behind the object and see a jet heading towards it. Then they both go behind
Then I look behind the object and see a jet heading towards it. Then they both go behind
some trees. I wait watching on the otherside, because there is a clearing. Then I realize
what i am looking at is not an UFO, but a jet. There were two jets. I was like oh it's a
jet. My middle child looked at me and said "What did you think it was a UFO? You know
they don't exist!" My son and I give her a look and said "You're right, they don't" (In order
to get some sleep at night I have told my two youngest that they don't exist, aliens and
all. I think they do but it's rare to see them, especially a UFO)

So i was disappointed that it wasn't a UFO but a Jet! I just wanted share because at one
point I thought it was something significant, and I was like finally!! But I was disappointed
once again! Why can't we see UFO's in MAINE!!!!

comes down to it, we still have to be honest with ourselves.

exception. Most sightings can easily be explained as I believe was the case in NY. When I
saw the first pictures posted I got quite excited but the thought did cross my mind that
they looked a little like yellowish balloons.

I was intrigued by the reports of strange lights over Manhattan and took a squiz at what

litmus test to show how gullible we really are.

We won't even see them coming. They will just uncloak themselves and put on a show of

posted on Oct, 14 2010 @ 06:58 AM                                                            link
Well I saw something really strange in the sky tonight. I was going for my usual walk,
around 8:30 p.m. I live in South Milwaukee, Wisconsin. It looked very much like some of
the objects in those videos. Basically a whitish yellow ball of light with a slight tail behind
it. The main ball of light seemed to contain collection of lots of little lights in the middle,
shimmering and moving around each other.

I live near the airport, and at first I nonchalantly thought it was just another plane coming
in for a landing. Either that or a runaway advertising balloon from one of the car lots near
me. It wasn't though. I realized it was neither once I really got a good look at it. It came
in from the west very suddenly, seeming to fade in as it came up from the horizon. It
followed a curved trajectory and moved very fast across half the sky leaving a short trail
behind it. Much too fast to be a plane or jet, and it was too close. The whole sighting was
maybe five to 10 seconds. It wasn't a meteor, I've seen those before, and this was WAY
too slow for that. It just kind of faded out and disappeared as it went over in a curve.

It was such a rare chance that I even saw it. I just happened to be facing the right
direction, and sat down on the curb to finish a song I was listening to in my iPhone before
I walked into my driveway. If I wouldn't have looked up just then, I never would have seen
it. Because I had my phones on I couldn't tell if there was any sound.

I have seen a few very strange things up north, where people have had a lot of UFO
sightings. This had the same look and movement to it as the objects I saw a few years
ago. I have no idea what it was. Funny thing though, I go on ATS tonight and find all these
threads about people seeing strange lighted objects in the night sky all over the
country.....! Who knows?

there's not enough evidence that these are alien craft, state that their OPINION is that

reply to post by OneNationUnder


     
     "I'm worried about the mass hysteria a few minor "sightings" can cause


What hysteria? Is anyone here hysterical? It, *hysteria*, is one of the buzz words used to
minimize & dismiss reports. That suggestion comes from Orson Wells famous, or
infamous, radio performance of War of the Worlds. Much of that report is urban legend
generated by the press to freak Wells & company out because the press, & other PTB,
thought the performance was irresponsible. Of all the sightings & reports I'm aware of, & I
have been paying attention, I've not seen one case of hysteria. In fact where mass
sightings have occured, such as those in Arizona, & Mexico, people seemed curious but
calm.

I can't say if the youtube videos cited are part of the10/13/10 prediction or not. I do
know if anyone was truly wigged out they have had the time to be so before now. We can
handle the truth.

I was thinking about this for the last 24 hrs, when all the activity started and I came to a
realization.

I realized the only reason we need disclosure is for those of us that have witnessed and
experienced such "craft/beings" is to have final and proven validation and an answer for
the world. Validation and truth in the face of skeptics and those who create an aura of
ridicule and put downs out of hate for those of us wanting the truth.

I have to question the motives of those who post endlessly that they do not exist.

It is a waiting game for us - a waiting game for that final slap in the face (Total
Disclosure) to those that have attacked, suppressed and lied in the face of those who have
a real experience and deep connection to that experience.

While I can see the passion of those (like me) who have seen them and want the ultimate
answer, I can't understand the passion and reason in those who have seen nothing involving
these unworldly vehicles, believe nothing involving them and for hours and hours, use
forums like this to attack, call names and stir dis-info to any tangible or possible visual
evidence.

So, I have a question.

Understandably: Those of us who have experienced a life altering and deep rooted
connection to our UFO/Anomaly encounter-experience and all the mental bells that go off
that tell us at that point that we are not alone in this vast and endless Universe.

And then you have

Those on here and everywhere else who deny any and all experiences - use any method in
their arsenal to discredit, attack, call names to end the discussion.

What do they gain?
Are they happy that they have put another week, month, year on real disclosure?
Happy they have driven the nail into a believer with a hammer of denial, insults and
attacks?
Paid to add some minute scientific rebuttal of said evidence - visual or physical?
What are the motives and reason behind those that deny any and all of this?

Over time - thousands upon thousands of sightings, experiences around the world. From
high ranking "military" personnel to the average citizen who lives in the back hills of China
- people everywhere speak out and exclaim that we are not alone.

So again - what are the motives behind those that deny?

Is it fear?

Debunkers, dis-info experts and skeptics - I ask you

Why are you on these forums all day, everyday, hours on end trying to fight and ridicule
something that hundreds of thousands claim they have seen, experienced for themselves?

What do you gain? What is the goal you wish to achieve?

Okay folks, I totally agree in this circumstance they for the time being are nothing more
than UFOs for they follow the full meaning of the term. They are in fact unidentified at
this time except for people speculation, they are most definitely flying as they are in the
air, and finally they are a form of object.

But I am also going to say that I have to agree with the fact that this does not fall under
the prophecy in any way. He said that we would know for sure what we had. I have one
major problem with the New York case and that is take a light up into the sky to about
the theoretical height the orbs were at and turn it on in the middle of the day and see if a
person could see it over the daylight. If you need a better description than watch an
airplane fly over head at night and you will see there navigation lights. Right? Now watch
that same plane in the day time and you won't see the lights at all but they are still turned
on. Or even go out and look at stars at night and then look in the day light. Our
atmosphere will defuse the light from them.

So with that said what everyone thought were lights in the day time sky would have had to
have been larger than that in order to be able to over come the light from the sun. Now I
am not saying they were balloons or alien craft but you look at the photos they are not
lights but they are colored objects. or they could very well but ultra reflective objects but
even with that they would be more blue than yellow and they would only reflect the light
from the sun where the light is originating.

The incident in China was already proven false if you look under that thread.

It is always fun to think you have seen things but you also must look at things objectively
when you analyze them afterword.

reply to post by rajaten

This eyewitness reports from New Yorkers are varied and some say emphatically they were
NOT balloons. I don't know what they saw, but here is a quote from page 24 of the related
mega 85+ page thread:

"I saw many of these white objects together with ohter passer bys at 17th Street & Park
Avenue at the NE corner of Union Square. I am a teacher at at nearby high school and not
a UFO freak, and never expected to see what I saw today. It was part fascinating,
worrysome and scary.
I ran into the school after a while and took pictures with my Canon EOS. I caught 2 of the
objects. But here is the strange thing. I saw at sparkly white with my eyes, but on the
photos they all appear dark.
I went into max resolution in my iPhoto and something amazing happens. in the same
frame in which my eyes from the ground were only able to see two of the objects my
camera caught about 10 more in the same frame that were not visible with my eyes. If
this is representative of what really happened today, there were not 50 over the sky of
manhattan but 500 or even more.

How can I post my pictures. I just signed up with this site to add my sightings of today. "

End Quote

What caught my interest is that this eye witness said the objects changed color after
photographing them and that there were more of them. I took his/her image and
enhanced it a bit and came up with this:

[atsimg]http://files.abovetopsecret.com/images/member/59ce6c7cbe5a.jpg[/atsimg]

As some of you know, some UFOs aren't visible to the naked eye but do show up in say
infrared. I was surprised to see over a dozen or more black orbs when only 2 were visible
to the naked eye and they were white by the way to the naked eye, not yellow. Is there
something unique to a Canon EOS's spectrum range and iPhoto that would account for this?
Note also that the additional orbs aren't just reflections of existing ones. I can't explain it,
maybe some photo camera buffs can? But there may have been many more objects that
were just seen by human eyes.

Well, just as an example of how easy it is to misinterpret what you are seeing: you claim
the Ohio picture is clearly a craft. However, if you look at it closely, you'll realize it's simply
a bird - there are countless pictures of birds posted here you could compare it to. So.. not
a craft.. just a bird.

I watched the NY livecam for awhile, and those lights kept coming at a regular interval.
They were all flying from the spot of your stills, and coming towards the camera, and
flying out of sight. i.e. regular flight patterns. Thusly planes. None of them exhibited any
sort of maneuvers that could NOT be explained away by our own flying craft.

Once you start discrediting many of those sensationalist videos, it becomes clear there was
not "mass sightings." Just an inordinate amount of UFO believers who scour the web that
day for videos to post. I don't think anything significant occurred.

headb
posted on Oct, 14 2010 @ 09:20 PM                                                        link

Listen up folks!
I rarely comment at all. I'd like to break wind now.

I rarely comment on my fellow skeptics because deep inside I am truly reserved. I am yet
to be convinced. Until I shake the hand of another being, I will not have to believe
anything. It's ok, I think that's still healthy.

[Argh! How typical! Keyboard messes up and erases my long post! Have to rewrite it again
I suppose.]

Healthy skepticism helps weed out good info from bad info and thus helps ATS somewhat.
But we all know about the resident debunkers and skeptics here. Personally, I find them
offensive at times. They seem to attack people psychologically and thus divert attention
from the subject at hand.

Sometimes I think I have yet to see them muse over an anomaly and acknowledge a gem
in some OP. It's always negative when you see these guys. It's as if, they were paid.

I mean, just because I'm still not convinced doesn't mean I have to pound on some witness.
I can keep my healthy skepticism and give the guy the benefit of the doubt. But I can see
personal attacks here, like the guy who said, "You really want to believe this, don't you?"
As a fellow skeptic, may I ask the same question to you? You really DON'T want to believe
this don't you?

Will your organization suffer from disclosure? Wait, on second thought, being a pro - you
might have no reservations on attacking others. Just get the job done. Whether your on a
government's payroll or TPTB's - it doesn't matter. You will continue debunking until there's
physical contact with other entities. I don't find that constructive criticism or skepticism at
all.

Your organization will however apologize to the most notable victim of your bashing as a
symbolic offering of peace. I'm thinking similar to the POPE saying sorry to Galileo after
300 years. But by then, the subject will not be as taboo as it is today. People will forget
how angry they were when you considered them to be ignorant fools who mistook balloons
for cars, or elephants, or planes for helicopters. What? People will forget. After 300yrs,
your org will still be alive and generations have passed.

Gasp! I mean I don't want to be like one of the guys in Medieval Europe who says, "You
idiot! The world is flat!" I must respect a person's intellect and hear him out. If he turns
out to be a nut, then I don't have to yell "You're a nut!" It wont help anyway.

I don't have to believe anything - this (ufology) is not a religion. I want to know. Let me
touch it and feel it then I'll decide whether or not it's real.

For those who are just truly skeptical and would like more physical proof - like me! -
please respect the views of other members. Bashing witnesses may discourage others to
come forward. Thanks for considering this!

I'm very skeptical, but there's a very sincere part of me that believes very strongly in the
possibility. However, instead of beating every unidentified light in the sky into the
ground and claiming it's aliens, I'd like to see real evidence. Unidentified is
unidentified, so a UFO is not evidence of aliens, and that's that. Until it's identified as
an actual alien spacecraft, it's just a UFO.

So, when I see so much hysteria over something yet unidentified being considered
aliens, I get a bit annoyed. People are using the term UFO to mean aliens, and it's
being interpreted that way, so now anything unidentified gets to be used as "evidence"
of aliens, which is total bull.

A village disappears after "UFO" events? I'd be surprised if it's true, first of all, and even
more surprised if none of those villagers are ever found after having moved down the
mountain, but even if they were totally gone out of thin air, where's the evidence that
aliens were behind it? Because there was something "Unidentified" there? That doesn't
fit!

Unidentified does not equal aliens, people!

Well, until there's a reasonable explanation it's a UFO. Balloons are a reasonable
explanation for some of the sightings, planes are a reasonable explanation for others.
But even if something can't be reasonably ruled out as a balloon or an airplane, that
doesn't automatically mean it's aliens, it could still very easily be a balloon or a plane,
or a cloud, or a bird, or one of a million other simple, terrestrial explanations.

Those explanations are boring though, so hey, why not aliens! Once it's decided that
something is aliens, you get to come up with why the aliens are here, what they want,
where they're from, and if that includes something like, say, visiting Earth thousands of
years ago, you even get to redefine people! You get to rewrite history in any way you
want, and it can't be disproven, because the aliens aren't here to tell their side of the
story. So you end up with thousands of conflicting alien stories when we don't even
have evidence of aliens themselves.
The problem comes along when you end up with something like what happened here,
with this thread. One small faction decides something, like a disclosure date, and
everyone piles in to accommodate it. Of course there is no real mass disclosure, so the
gaping hole of logic has to be filled, and quickly, with whatever may be at hand. This
time it's airplanes above an airport. Before it was telepathic communication about how
the governments of the world wouldn't allow it, or how the different alien races were
warring with each other and wouldn't allow it, and so on. It turns into a huge mess, yet
every single time there are people out there who step up to defend it as if it's perfectly
logical.

overextended1
posted on Oct, 15 2010 @ 02:29 AM                                                            link

This whole thing on UFO's, spaceships, mother ships, abductions, secret alien bases, and
"eye-witness accounts of working on "secret programs" etc., bring all of us to absolutely
NO conclusion. Sure. We can speculate on what we think it may be. We can reminisce on
an experience that we had in our back yard; but one way or another, as a civilization we
will not believe a thing until it's at our doorstep. We constantly bicker back and forth
about balloons, our own Military, etc., that there will NEVER be any conclusive evidence.

How many people have come on this forum saying that they were some informant, or
"inside guy"? (I can't even count how many), as I have been on this forum for MANY years,
to see that NOTHING has come to be. (No offense to the OP).

With that said; we are at a stalemate when it comes to UFO, or Alien visitation. Unless
something hovers over the White House and/or major cities, we are going to bickering
back and forth on what is CGI, weather balloons, or the possibility that it may be real.
What it really comes down to is if they're not knocking on our front freaking door, there
will always be skepticism.

I refuse to believe that little lights in the sky are indicative of ET life. Do I believe that
there is ET life? Absolutely. I'd be a fool to say otherwise.

The problem that we have now, is that with technology, we have idiots that post vids on
our site that our fake, or what seems to be fake. Most of our members are becoming so
very skeptic that, personally, I don't blame them. I am part of that crowd.

Anyway, take it for what you will. Balloons, Military Craft, UFO's, Mother-Ships..........

One way or another........it doesn't matter until they're knocking on your front door, or
blowing up your Lexus.

-OE

benevolent would step in and stop the madness.

wackos were spotting jacko everywhere , and when elvis died , people were seeing him
working in the local chip-shop.

incoherent babblings and wafflings of people who see a balloon and , immediately , jump
all over it like white on rice , claiming the space brothers and the ashtar galactic
command are putting on an aeronautical display , before landing on the whitehouse lawn
and announcing the cure to end all disease.

The whole subject is tarnished with ridicule and uncorroborated , unsubstantiated
nonsense now.

Summary: The modern phenomena of UFOs and “flying saucers” began in Washington
state on June 24, 1947, when Kenneth Arnold spotted nine mysterious, high-speed objects
“flying like a saucer would” along the crest of the Cascade Range near Mount Rainier. His
report made international headlines and triggered hundreds of similar accounts of “flying
saucers” locally and across the nation.
They were flat and rather heel-shaped, very shiny, and they moved erratically, like a
"saucer would if you skipped it across water." You can see Arnold's drawing of what he saw
here. Arnold estimated their size at about two-thirds that of a DC-4, and he calculated
their speed at over 1500 mph by timing their travel between two mountain peaks of
known distance.
